[x] MOB-93 [BUG] Vendor BUY/SELL cards would not scroll unless the drag started in the gap between cards (BUG-032)
	- FORM (2026-11-24): FORM (imported 2026-11-23; sent 11/22/2026 22:10:26) | Bug - something broke | The sliding of the buy/sell menu at the vendor would glitch and be mostly unresponsive. | before: Interacting with the vendor. | impact: Annoying but playable | fun: 5/5 | device: Galaxy S25+, firefox, installed: Yes | v1.0.0 build 1.0.0-cc004cfc1f | floor 45 | The DIY Dad | 414x812 @2.61x portrait, phone layout, installed. KBP: It seems I have to touch the slim black space between the item cards to scroll. 
	- Device: Galaxy S25+, Firefox, installed; v1.0.0 build 1.0.0-cc004cfc1f; 414x812 @2.61x portrait, phone layout; floor 45; The DIY Dad
	- Summary: Built and verified in the folder, awaiting your push and phone check. On phones the vendor's BUY and SELL cards no longer use a 3D flip; they flip with a short flat squash, so a drag that starts on a card should scroll the list. Look of the cards at rest is unchanged.
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run): Triaged from the FORM line with your KBP detail (scrolling only works when the touch starts in the thin black gap between cards). Cause, as far as can be told without Firefox: the shop cards (MOB-53) reuse the Bestiary flip cards, which are 3D (perspective on the card, preserve-3d on the inner layer, hidden backfaces); Firefox for Android has trouble starting a touch scroll on 3D-transformed content inside a scrolling sheet, while the gap between cards has no 3D content. Emulated Chromium scrolls from a card on the old build too, so the failure itself was not reproduced here (Firefox is not installed in the test container). Fix, shop only (the Bestiary and Codex cards are untouched): ShatteredDadAdventure.html adds #ovShop rules that turn off the 3D context and swap front/back with visibility; js/ui.js marks a tapped shop card (class m93t) so only the tapped card plays the 0.24 s squash (none when the phone asks for reduced motion; none when the shop rebuilds after a purchase). The Codex flip cards (MOB-14) use the same 3D cards; if they scroll badly on your phone too, add a KBP note and the same change can go there.
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run): Verification performed: node --check on all js; 7/7 class clears (one class per process); sw.js re-stamped (BUILD 1.0.0-f8c0478692 -> 1.0.0-92c700770c) + --check. Playwright (Chromium) old vs new build on emulated iPhone SE, iPhone 13, Pixel 5 and iPhone 13 landscape, BUY and SELL tabs: elements with a 3D context inside the shop 32 (BUY) / 40 (SELL) -> 0; touch drag starting on a card scrolls 105 px and in the gap 105 px on both builds; a drag does not flip a card; tap flips, tap on the card top unflips, the back's BUY button opens the purchase confirm; the flip animation runs on the tapped card only and not under reduced motion; shop screenshots at rest identical by eye; no page errors. Desktop 1280x800 (title, play frame, shop list) md5-identical to the old build. Protected screens on iPhone SE + iPhone 13: cover NEW GAME opens the deck, deck swipes, flips and Choose opens the class confirm, discovered Bestiary card flips, undiscovered shows "?" and does not flip; all 4 screenshots per device md5-identical to the old build. Folder md5 = working copy. Real phones and Firefox not tested: your Galaxy S25+ check is the real test.
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run): sw.js is also in the [c] MOB-54 PUSH block; pushing either block commits sw.js with tonight's stamp. Push both together (the combined block in the run report) so the stamp matches the pushed files.
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run 2): MOB-60 (built tonight, [c]) also changed ShatteredDadAdventure.html (COMPANION tooltips only; the #ovShop CSS is untouched) and re-stamped sw.js. Push all three items together with the combined block in tonight's report.
	- Files: ShatteredDadAdventure.html, js/ui.js, sw.js

[x] MOB-60 [CONTENT] Give allies armor and weapons, carry slots by size, drop all on death; tap the ally for orders and inventory
	- KBP (2026-11-15): Add the ability to give your ally armor and weapons; Allies should even have carry slots but smaller allies have fewer slots than bigger allies; when the ally dies all items are dropped where they died; control of the ally and their inventory can be initiated by scanning tapping on the ally which initiates a pop-up for giving direction or ally inventory.
	- Device: not stated
	- Summary: Built and verified in the folder, awaiting your push and phone check. Tap (or click) your companion to open its card: HP, ATK and DEF with gear bonuses in [brackets], stance, what it is wearing and its bag, plus stance buttons, "Give them something..." and "Take something back...". A companion wears one weapon plus head, torso, legs and feet armor (no charms); its bag holds 2 (Little Timmy), 4 (Chad, Sharon, Uncle Ron) or 6 (Officer Doug) items. Handing over or taking back costs no turn and needs the companion on one of the 8 tiles next to Dad. Cursed gear sticks to the companion. When the companion falls, everything it wore or carried drops on its tile.
	- Note (2026-11-15, nightly run): Today the companion (game.ally) has fixed ALLIES stats (hp/atk/def), no equipment and no bag; orders come from the COMPANION button (openStanceMenu) and Shift-click/orderAlly. Marked [!]: new ally equipment and bag fields in the run save (sda_save_v4 must still load; old saves get an empty kit), combat math using ally gear (js/game.js), a give/take sheet (HTML + js/ui.js), tapping the ally on the map (js/input.js canvasTap; SCAN taps must still identify), drops on ally death (loot at the ally's tile) and carry-slot sizes per ally in js/data.js (3+ systems, save field). Proposed unless you add a KBP note: slots by size (the kid 2, most adults 4, Officer Doug 6), allies can wear head/torso/legs/feet and one weapon, no charms; cursed gear sticks to them too; giving or taking costs no turn while adjacent.
	- Note (2026-11-23, nightly run): MOB-84 (built tonight, [c]) adds "Give to Companion" in the bag for identified healing items (companion next to Dad; costs a turn). The ally pop-up planned here should offer the same giving and reuse giveToAlly() in js/game.js.
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run 2): Worked tonight as the one [!] item (no [-] items waiting; no BUG or UX [!] ready; MOB-27 waits on the MOB-26/MOB-19 box object, MOB-37 builds on MOB-54's genFloor change, which is still waiting for your push and phone check, and MOB-45 waits on your review, so MOB-60 was the lowest CONTENT item ready). Built per the proposal in the 2026-11-15 note. js/data.js: ALLIES[].carry (Timmy 2, Doug 6, others 4). js/game.js MOB-60 block after giveToAlly: allyKit/allyBag (game.ally.kit {weapon,head,torso,legs,feet}, game.ally.bag [{it,qty}], stacking like Dad's bag), allyAtk/allyDefStat (base + worn gear ATK/DEF, affixes included) now used by allyHit and allySufferAdjacent, allyGive (wearable gear goes on; the piece it replaces goes to the companion's bag, else Dad's bag, else the floor; other items go in the bag; one item from a stack), allyTake (worn piece or a whole bag stack back to Dad's bag; refuses cursed gear and a full bag), allyFall (both companion death paths: the defeat line, then every worn and carried item dropped on its tile, or the next tile if that is a staircase), openAllyCard (comic card in the ATTRIBUTES style) with _m60GiveList ("Wear:" / "Carry:" per item; known healing items also offer "Feed:", which is MOB-84's giveToAlly and still costs a turn) and _m60TakeList. saveRun adds kit and bag to the ally; old sda_save_v4 saves load with an empty kit and bag. openStanceMenu (COMPANION button, MORE drawer, Tool Belt, O key) now opens the card; the stance buttons are on it. js/input.js canvasTap: a tap or click on the visible following companion opens the card instead of stepping onto its tile; an armed SCAN tap still only identifies, and Shift+click still gives orders. ShatteredDadAdventure.html: the two COMPANION button tooltips now say "Companion: stance, gear and bag". Your line said "scanning tapping on the ally"; read as a plain tap (SCAN-armed taps keep their identify pop-up). Companions do not use items from their bag on their own; add a KBP note if they should (for example, eat a healing item when low).
	- Note (2026-11-24, nightly run 2): Verification performed: node --check on all js; vm unit test of the new functions 38/38 (base stats, bag sizes 2/4/6, wear and swap, one item from a stack, no turn used, refused when not adjacent, cursed weapon cannot be taken back or swapped, full bag refused, save and Continue keep kit and bag and the gear ATK, an old save without kit/bag loads, card and give list build, allyFall and a combat death drop every item on the companion's tile); 7/7 class clears (one class per process; classes 0-3 rerun on the final game.js); sw.js re-stamped (BUILD 1.0.0-92c700770c -> 1.0.0-82ef4f23ad) + --check. Playwright (cloud Chromium), old vs new build, iPhone SE, iPhone 13, Pixel 5, iPhone 13 landscape, 58/58: old build: tapping the companion steps Dad onto its tile (no card); new build: the tap opens the card with no turn and no move, no horizontal overflow, Give > Wear puts a weapon on (ATK shown with [+bonus]), Take gives it back, COMPANION opens the card, an armed SCAN tap opens no card, no page errors; desktop 1280x800: a click on the companion and the O key open the card. Desktop 1280x800 title and play frame (no companion) md5-identical to the old build. New tools/tools_make_loops.js (needs ffmpeg): decodes each source mono 32 kHz, estimates the beat (onset autocorrelation, 60-140 BPM), takes a whole number of 4-beat bars near 24 s, picks the start (after the intro) whose surroundings best match the point one loop later, blends the 0.35 s after the loop end into the start (equal power) so the end runs into the beginning, evens out loudness (RMS about -17 dBFS, peak cap), and writes assets/music/<biome>-<role>.mp3 (mono 40 kbps); --check confirms every have:true entry has its file, a len, at most 200 KB each and at most 4.6 MB in all. The loops were cut on your computer (ffmpeg there); the source folder is read only. js/data.js: have:true and len (exact loop seconds) on 29 entries, three titles name the versions used, lowe rare stays have:false. js/audio.js: a rare roll whose loop is missing falls back to main (before, it would have gone silent); the player loops exactly len seconds (loopStart/loopEnd) and skips any MP3 lead-in a browser leaves in. sw.js: The House loops added to ASSETS; other loops go to a separate cache (sda-music-1, kept across updates, cache-first) the first time they play; re-stamped 1.0.0-896a2c4453 -> 1.0.0-cc004cfc1f (41 files). tools/tools_sw_stamp.js hashes .mp3 files as binary (before, it read every non-image file as text). js/ui.js: credit line at the bottom of The Dad Manual (built from the MUSIC table). docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md: Music credits section. 	  Today: Dad has statuses in game.statuses (saved with the run); creatures have only stun, root and blind counters (js/game.js applyTo); throwables carry at most one status (Warm Beer Can and Frozen Burger Patty: soaked; Entertainment Center Knot: root); traps carry one status each; no weapon or armor has a damage type or resistance; render.js has blast, pulse and proj effects only. 	  P1. Six damage types (a hit can carry one; the type's effect lands on a proc roll):
	    BURNING ("On Fire, Technically"): 2 dmg per turn for 3 turns. Put out at once by Soaked, a water tile, a Wet Floor trap, or drinking a Warm Water Bottle. Cancels Chilled.
	    SHOCKED ("Static Discharge"): loses its next turn (1 turn). If the target is Soaked: 2 turns and +2 dmg on the hit. No cure needed; it passes.
	    CHILLED ("Cold Shoulder"): acts every other turn for 3 turns. A third chill while still Chilled makes it Frozen Solid for 2 turns (cannot move, can still attack; the same rule as the MOB-70 Frozen fix). Burning cancels it; Gas Station Coffee cures it. The Unskippable Ad's "Frozen (Update Installing)" stays as it is (the software joke).
	    BLEEDING ("Paper Cut, Spiritually"): 1 dmg per turn for 5 turns; stacks to 3 (up to 3 dmg per turn). Any healing item stops it.
	    SOAKED (already exists): Dad -DEF as today; creatures blinded 1 turn as today. New: it puts out Burning and doubles Shocked.
	    PSYCHIC ("Per My Previous Email"): ignores DEF from armor; 25% chance each turn for 3 turns that the target spends its turn second-guessing itself. Reduced by WIS (each WIS point above 3 takes 1 turn off). Cured by using a Dadism.
	  P2. Proc chances: weapons with a type proc on 25% of hits (powered weapons 30%); throwables and traps with a type always apply it. Bosses: every duration halved (round down, minimum 1).
	  P3. Weapon types (first pass): Burning: Mini Blowtorch, The Flame Spatula, Backyard Tiki Torch. Shocked: Extension Cord of Endless Horizons, Orbital Sander of Reckoning. Chilled: Snow Shovel of Seasonal Vengeance. Bleeding: Pizza Cutter of a Thousand Slices, Orbital Edger of Property-Line Wars, The Great PTO Reaper. Soaked: Garden Hose of Unspooling Doom, Pressure Wand of Aquatic Judgment. All others stay plain.
	  P4. Throwables: Frozen Burger Patty becomes Chilled (was soaked); Warm Beer Can stays Soaked; Entertainment Center Knot stays root. Traps: Tangle of Extension Cords gains Shocked; Scattered Building Bricks gains Bleeding (keeps The Knee); HOA Violation Notice becomes Psychic (keeps Hangry); Wet Floor, Brown Substance and Dog Walker Special stay Soaked.
	  P5. Creature attacks (by lore; about 1 in 4 creatures, the rest stay plain): Burning: Lawnmower Peak, Birthday Party, Sample Station. Shocked: Rogue Robo-Vac, Self-Checkout Sentinel, Motion Sensor Light. Chilled: Thermostat Phantom, The Waterboy. Bleeding: Paint Swatch Swarm, Assembly Instructions, Off-Leash Dog. Psychic: The Covenant, Violation Notice, Annual Meeting, Hall Monitor, Lingering Board Member, Nextdoor Post. Creature attacks proc on 20% of hits.
	  P6. Creature resistances and weaknesses (lore): immune to Bleeding: Rogue Robo-Vac, Shopping Cart, Aisle 12 Forklift, Sale Sign Golem, Fog Machine, Trophy Case; immune to Soaked: Mrs. Kowalski's Fish (weak to Shocked); weak to Burning (double duration): paper creatures (Permission Slip, Permission Form, Assembly Instructions, Neighborhood Newsletter, Violation Notice, The Check, Children's Menu); immune to Psychic: Morning Obligation and The Wait (they have seen worse).

Shocked: Rubber Dishwashing Gloves, Crocs (Sport Mode Engaged). Chilled: Winter Beanie, Windbreaker, Lumberjack Flannel. Bleeding: Chest Pad, Elbow Pads, Steel-Toe Boots, Relaxed-Fit Jeans. Soaked: Emergency Rain Poncho (immune by itself). Psychic: Hearing Protection Muffs, Wraparound Sunglasses. Shown on the item card ("Resists: Burning").

	  P9. Animations (js/render.js, one new "aura" effect kind drawn around the affected tile while the effect lasts; sprite and ASCII modes): Burning: small orange flames flicker up from the base. Shocked: two-frame yellow zigzag sparks. Chilled: pale-blue frost ring with a slow snowflake tick; Frozen Solid adds a blue tint. Bleeding: red drips fall from the sprite each turn. Soaked: blue droplets. Psychic: purple wavy rings with a small "..." bubble. In ASCII mode the glyph is tinted in the type's color. The effect skips frames when reduced motion is set.
	  P10. Text and UI: new STATUSES entries for Dad (BURN, ZAP, CHILL, FROZEN SOLID, BLEED, DOUBT icons in the status bar), log lines in the deadpan voice (for example: "The Flame Spatula proves OSHA right. The Sale Sign Golem is on fire, technically."), one line of encouragement when Dad shakes off a long effect ("You walked it off. The kids saw. Findings in the current table (js/data.js RECIPES, 34 recipes, all ingredient ids valid):
	    F1. Real brand names in player-facing recipe text (shown on the Codex recipe cards): "WD-40 Torch" and "WD-40 + Blowtorch." / "Propane + WD-40" / "WD-40 + Horseshoe." (the item itself is "Can of WG-40 Spray Lube"); "Gatorade + Pedialyte." and "Coffee + Blue Gatorade." 	  PROPOSED SYSTEM (tiers, hints, record):
	    S1. Tier 1 = two non-crafted items: can be tried any time, with or without the video. Tier 2 = a crafted result + an item: cannot be tried until its YallTube video is found (the partner never appears in the picker before then; a desktop C-key attempt logs "You have not seen the video. You are not doing this without the video.").
	    S2. Hints: every craftable item gets 1-3 workshop tags: TAPE (duct tape, zip ties), FUEL (propane, WG-40), POWER (both batteries, HDMI, extension cord, blowtorch), FASTENER (screws), LUMBER (plywood, broom handle), GRILL (spatula, burger patty, hot dog, cold pizza, apron), CAFFEINE (coffee, energy drink, mints), FLUIDS (blue drink, kids' solution, juice pouch, coconut water, hose water, red drink), MEDS (both painkillers, antacids, ginger ale, menthol rub, Epsom salt, glucosamine), SNACK (banana, protein bar, crockpot stew), SPORTS (golf ball, bowling ball, horseshoe), POCKET (charms), and LAYER (any two garments for the same slot). Two items that share a tag "might combine": the BAG detail shows a small wrench and "Might combine with: <names of carried partners>", and only those appear in the MOB-41 picker. Some tag-sharing pairs make nothing on purpose, so a hint is a hint, not a promise.
	    S3. Result display: a known recipe shows its result; an untried pair shows "???"; a pair already tried that makes nothing shows the big X. Trying a pair that makes nothing costs a turn but no items ("Those two do not combine. He tried. The garage saw."). A failed 30% roll still learns the recipe (the "Learning Experience" now teaches something).
	    S4. Save: one new run-save field, triedPairs (list of "a+b" keys that made nothing); old sda_save_v4 saves load with an empty list. knownRecipes stays as is. No new localStorage key.
	    S5. YallTube video: reveals one unknown recipe as now, picking a tier 2 recipe half the time once at least one of its crafted ingredients has been made this run, so videos stay useful late in a run.
	  TIER 1 ADDITIONS (26; existing items only; stats in line with the current table):

Reach rule A, as you chose: a reach weapon hits any visible enemy within its reach in any direction (counted like a king's move, so 3 east and 1 south is 3 tiles away) when the straight line to it is clear; walls, closed doors, the vendor, fountains and other enemies in between block. The phone target box lists those enemies (tap to strike, swipe to choose); on desktop, clicking an enemy in reach strikes it. Pressing a direction keeps the old straight-line strike.

	- Summary: Fixed. The Codex shows only what you have met. Creatures and Bosses: as in the Bestiary (ever encountered). Consumables: identified this run. 	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run 5): js/game.js: new gearScore / gearWeights / pickGearTpl next to pickLoot. Strength: weapons = ATK + half the level requirement; armor and charms = DEF + ATK + PER + WIS + half the carry bonus. Each piece is ranked within its list (0 weakest, 1 strongest) and weighted (1 - rank x (1 - d))^3, never below 0.02, where d = (floor - 1) / 99. pickLoot is the only gear picker for floor loot and drops; vendors use fixed stock lists and were not touched. Constants LOOT_TIER_EXP (3) and LOOT_TIER_MIN (0.02) are at the top of the block if you want it steeper or flatter.

[x] MOB-30 [CONTENT] Comedy-break random encounters (no game effect), 5 per biome
	- KBP (2026-11-10): Add whimsical random encounters that have no bearing on the game, but are a simple comedy break. Give me 5 suggestions per biome.
	- Device: not stated
	- Summary: Fixed. All 50 comedy-break encounters (5 per biome, kept as written) now turn up on the story card, headed "<Biome> · A BRIEF INTERLUDE", with one "Carry on." button: no game effect, no turn spent. How often is rolled once per run from the run seed, anywhere from 1 floor in 10 to 1 floor in 3. Never on boss floors (10, 20 ... 100), never while a creature is in view (the family dog does not count), at most one per floor, no repeats within a run; it shows 4-15 turns after arriving on a floor, once Dad has stopped walking and no other card or screen is open. Seeded and Daily runs get the same encounters on the same floors. Floor 1 is included (add a KBP note to leave floor 1 to the planned MOB-45 tutorial).
	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run): Proposed mechanics: at most one per floor, on about 1 floor in 6, never on boss floors and never with an enemy in view; shown on the story card with one "Carry on" button; costs no turn; drawn from its own random stream so seeded and Daily floors stay the same. Several are the scattered dad encouragement the tone rules ask for (House 3, Park 5, Soccer 5, School 2, HOA 5). Suggestions:
		The House: (1) A single sock lies in the hallway. Its partner is not with it. Dad observes a moment of silence and moves on. (2) The smoke detector chirps once, from a location science cannot determine. It does not chirp again while he is listening. (3) A child's drawing is taped to the wall: Dad, taller than the house, holding a spatula like a sword. He stands a little straighter. (4) The junk drawer offers him a rubber band, a dead battery and a key to nothing. He declines all three, then takes the key. (5) Someone has set the thermostat to 74. There is no one else here. The investigation is ongoing.
		Lowe & Behold: (1) An employee in an orange-adjacent apron says "Let me know if you need anything" and is never seen again by anyone. (2) Dad finds the exact bolt he needs, in a bin labeled for a different bolt. He does not question a gift. (3) A man in Aisle 12 is also holding a broken part and looking around. They nod. No words are needed. (4) The paint-mixing machine shakes for forty seconds for no customer at all. He watches the whole thing. It is the best part of his day. (5) He pulls the cord on a display mower, just to feel something. It does not start. He feels something anyway.
		The Park: (1) A goose stands in the path and stares. He takes the long way. The goose has won, and both of them know it. (2) Another dad on a bench shares a sunflower seed and a weather forecast. Both are accurate. They part as brothers. (3) A frisbee lands at his feet. He throws it back with perfect form. It goes somewhere else entirely. A child applauds anyway. (4) The playground water fountain works on the first try. Nobody will believe him. (5) A bench plaque reads "In memory of a dad who always found the good parking spot." He sits for a moment. He has been that dad.
		Vastco: (1) A sample lady offers a toothpick of something. He does not know what it was. He would buy forty pounds of it. (2) He lifts a 48-pack of batteries, remembers the 48-pack at home, and puts it back. Growth. (3) A flatbed cart with one wobbly wheel follows him for three aisles. He did not push it. It has chosen him. (4) A man demonstrates a blender that can blend a phone. Dad watches the whole thing, nodding. He did not need a blender. (5) The hot dog combo still costs a dollar fifty. Somewhere, the universe remains in balance.
		Soccer Complex: (1) Another parent asks which kid is his. He points. It is the wrong kid. Both kids wave. (2) The halftime orange slices arrive. For one moment, everyone is eight years old again. (3) A referee asks him to stop coaching from the sideline. He was not coaching. He was explaining the offside rule to himself. (4) A folding chair refuses to unfold. He wins in round three. A nearby dad gives him a slow nod of respect. (5) His kid finds him on the sideline mid-game and gives a thumbs-up. Nothing else on this floor matters.
		The School: (1) The lost-and-found holds three of his jackets. He did not know they were missing. He takes one and leaves two for later. (2) A hallway poster reads "You are doing better than you think." It is meant for the students. He takes it personally, in a good way. (3) The intercom crackles: "Would the owner of a silver minivan..." Every dad in the building checks his pockets. (4) A science-fair volcano erupts in an empty classroom. Nobody set it off. He gives it a respectful B-plus. (5) The pen at the front desk is chained to the desk. He understands. He would chain it too.
		The Family Restaurant: (1) The staff sing happy birthday at a nearby table. Dad hums along, slightly off key, fully committed. (2) A kids' menu with crayons lies on a table. He completes the maze, then the word search. He does not stop until it is finished. (3) Two other dads reach for the same check at the same moment. Neither moves. The standoff is still going when he leaves. (4) A booth seat makes a noise as he sits. He clarifies, to no one, that it was the seat. (5) The bread basket refills itself while he is not looking. He has learned not to ask questions here.
		IDKEA: (1) He finds a little pencil. He already has nineteen. This one feels different. (2) A showroom couch is so comfortable that he closes his eyes for "one second." The narrator tactfully looks away for eleven minutes. (3) An arrow on the floor points both ways. He follows it. It is the correct choice. It is also the wrong one. (4) A cardboard family of four smiles in a fake kitchen. He waves. He hopes they are happy. (5) The meatball smell finds him from three sections away. He has no plans to eat. His legs have made plans of their own.
		The Neighborhood: (1) A neighbor waves from his lawn. Dad waves back. They have waved for nine years and neither knows the other's name. (2) The ice cream truck plays its song three streets over. Every child in the area freezes like a deer. So does he. (3) A sprinkler catches him exactly once, then turns away as if nothing happened. (4) A garage door across the street opens on a man staring into his own garage, then closes. Dad knows that man. Dad is that man. (5) Two dads stand over an open car hood, touching nothing. He joins them. Nothing is fixed. Everyone feels better.
		The HOA Dominion: (1) A flyer on a lamppost announces a meeting about the flyers on the lampposts. (2) A perfectly edged lawn gleams. He takes a moment to appreciate it, one craftsman to another, even here. (3) One mailbox is the approved beige. The next is a slightly different beige. A letter has already been sent. (4) A sign reads "Please keep off the grass." A smaller sign beneath it reads "Please keep off the sign." (5) A note in his pocket, in his kid's handwriting: "Go get em Dad. You are the best at this." He does not remember putting it there.

	- KBP (2026-11-10): The screen seems to flicker while moving. I want that to stop. 
	- Device: Galaxy S25+ (KBP 2026-11-10)
	- Summary: Fixed. With MOTION on, the light around Dad no longer flickers while he auto-travels: the shading that marks what he can see now moves with him during each step, the same as with MOTION off. The glides, walk frames and the soft light itself are unchanged, and nothing changes while he stands still. BUG-018 resolved.
	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run 4): Logged BUG-018 (open). Tried: Playwright iPhone 13, iPhone SE and Galaxy S25+ profiles, 8 steps while sampling the map canvas every animation frame: no blank or dark single frames, brightness steady, the canvas is never resized during a move. Things that change on screen during a step and could read as flicker on a real phone: the 80 ms glide and walk frames (M15/N29, MOTION setting), the soft lighting around Dad (turned off automatically on slow phones, which changes the look once), and wall edges redrawn as new tiles come into view.

	  -> Actioned 2026-11-10: found and fixed in js/render.js (new _jzMask); item set to [c], BUG-018 resolved. Details below.
	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run 5): Cause: with MOTION on, each step glides Dad and the camera over 80 ms, but the field of view (which tiles are dimmed and which get the soft light) switched to his new tile at once. On screen the lit area jumped a tile ahead of Dad at the start of every step and slid back during the glide; auto-travel takes a step every 120 ms, so the edge of the light pulsed about 8 times a second. MOTION off has no glide, so the light and Dad moved together. Fix: js/render.js _jzMask(): while Dad is gliding, the dim/light layer is drawn in a second pass shifted by his glide offset, so it travels with him; frames at rest take the old drawing path unchanged.

[x] MOB-35 [BUG] Hunger and Thirst stopped working after Continue; label turns red at 90% (BUG-014)
	- KBP (2026-11-10): Hunger and thirst do not seem to be consistently working. On a play through, my character had full bars and no damage and consuming food and drink did not affect them. Additionally, when Hunger or Thirst are close to causing damage (90% there) the text should turn red for which ever is at that point.
	- Device: not stated
	- Summary: Fixed. After Continue (including reopening the app, which resumes the auto-save) both meters were broken: drawn full, never hurt, food and drink did nothing. They now work after Continue, and the Hunger or Thirst label turns red (bold) once its meter is 90% full. Remaining: Continue restarts both meters at empty, because the save never stored them; see the last note.
	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run): Cause: saveRun() never stored the meter values and continueRun() never set them, so after Continue they were NaN: the bar width became "NaN%" (ignored by the browser, so the bar drew full), NaN never reaches the damage threshold, and eating or drinking left NaN. New runs were fine until the first Continue. Fix: js/game.js continueRun starts Hunger at 0 and Thirst at the class start value; survivalTick resets any broken meter to 0; js/ui.js _survLbl() turns the label red at 90% or more (CSS #statline .surv-warn in ShatteredDadAdventure.html). Logged BUG-014 (resolved).

[x] MOB-29 [BUG] Zooming in on phones pushed the buttons off the screen (BUG-013)
	- KBP (2026-11-10): On Galaxy S25+, when zooming in, The buttons are affected and I have trouble accessing some of them, even when going to my bag or junk drawer. Zooming should only affect the map area and nothing else.
	- Device: Galaxy S25+ (orientation and version not stated)
	- Summary: Fixed. On phones and tablets, zooming in (pinch on the map, or ZOOM + in the junk drawer) now shows fewer, larger tiles inside the same map box; the HUD, the action bar, BAG and the junk drawer stay where they are and the page no longer grows past the screen. Pinching outside the map no longer zooms the whole page. Zooming out (MOB-04) and desktop zoom are unchanged.
	- Note (2026-11-10, nightly run): Cause: on phones fitCanvas() (js/config.js) multiplied the map canvas size by the zoom factor, so the map grew taller than its slot, the page overflowed, and the browser shrank the page to fit it, pushing BAG/SCAN/WAIT/THROW/MORE below the screen (also while BAG or the drawer was open). The same overflow caused BUG-009 (the top zoom step flipped the grid to 11x15). The page also allowed the browser's own pinch-zoom. Fix: js/config.js fitCanvas: on compact layouts zoom-in divides the tile grid by the zoom factor (portrait 9x13 -> 7x10 -> 6x9 ... -> 3x4 at the top step; landscape 15x10 -> 5x3) and keeps the canvas in its box; ShatteredDadAdventure.html viewport adds maximum-scale=1, user-scalable=no; js/input.js blocks the iOS Safari page pinch (gesturestart). 	- Device: not stated
	- Summary: Fixed (reworked after your 2026-11-09 note). Two fingers on the map zoom it: pinch together to zoom out, spread apart to zoom in (one step per 25% change in finger spread). Zooming out past the normal view now shows more of the floor instead of stopping: on a portrait phone the view widens from 9x13 tiles through 14x20, 20x29 and 29x42 to the whole floor (40 tiles across, the floor centered with black above and below); landscape and desktop widen from 15x10 to the whole floor (45x30).
